 17475	
 17476	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
 17477	# dispatch-lens(Projects/派工鏡頭注入_計劃,2026-09-03):代碼審派工詞的固定席鏡頭
 17478	# hook 是薄殼(scripts/hooks/claude/dispatch-lens-hook.py),所有 git/合約/圖譜判斷都在這裡,
 17479	# 沿用既有 helper(_git_commit_exists / INVARIANT_RE 家族 / _vault_in 的排序規則)。
 17480	# 消毒原則(r2/r3 載荷席):來自圖譜或 diff 的自由文字零輸出——只印固定字彙、base 已追蹤路徑、
 17481	# base 版合約行(KEY: 行)。清單本身仍用工作樹圖譜算(d3:能藏不能說)。
 17482	# rc:0=成功(含 0 篇,text 空)/2=輸入或 git 壞/3=vault 缺/4=base 不在主線
 17483	# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
 17484	_LENS_HEADER = "lumos 自動附加:本次改動的固定席節點(來源 impact --diff,只供參考)"
 17485	_LENS_SHA_RE = re.compile(r"^[0-9a-f]{40}$|^[0-9a-f]{64}$")
 17486	_LENS_KIND = {"direct": "直接相依", "indirect": "間接相依", "incident": "事故"}
 17487	
 17488	
 17489	def _lens_range_ok(diff_range):
 17490	    """恰好一個 `..`、兩端非空、不含 `...`、不以 - 開頭(r3 極端輸入席收緊)。"""
 17491	    if not isinstance(diff_range, str) or diff_range.startswith("-") or "..." in diff_range:
 17492	        return None
 17493	    parts = diff_range.split("..")
 17494	    if len(parts) != 2 or not parts[0] or not parts[1]:
 17495	        return None
 17496	    if any(ch.isspace() for ch in diff_range):
 17497	        return None
 17498	    return parts[0], parts[1]
 17499	
 17500	
 17501	def _lens_git(repo_root, *args, quote=False):
 17502	    import subprocess as _sp
 17503	    cmd = ["git", "-C", str(repo_root)]
 17504	    if quote:
 17505	        cmd += ["-c", "core.quotePath=false"]
 17506	    try:
 17507	        return _sp.run(cmd + list(args), capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=20)
 17508	    except (OSError, _sp.TimeoutExpired):
 17509	        return None
 17510	
 17511	
 17512	def _lens_full_sha(repo_root, rev):
 17513	    r = _lens_git(repo_root, "rev-parse", "--verify", "-q", f"{rev}^{{commit}}")
 17514	    if r is None or r.returncode != 0:
 17515	        return None
 17516	    sha = r.stdout.strip()
 17517	    return sha if _LENS_SHA_RE.match(sha) else None
 17518	
 17519	
 17520	def _mainline_ref(repo_root):
 17521	    """主線 tip:本地 main/master 的 upstream 優先(r3:remote 未必叫 origin),沒設 upstream 才用本地分支。
 17522	    回 (ref 名, sha) 或 None。"""
 17523	    for cand in ("main@{upstream}", "master@{upstream}", "main", "master"):
 17524	        sha = _lens_full_sha(repo_root, cand)
 17525	        if sha:
 17526	            return cand, sha
 17527	    return None
 17528	
 17529	
 17530	def _lens_cache_path(repo_root, base_sha, head_sha):
 17531	    import hashlib
 17532	    # repo 部分用 realpath(跟 armed 檔的 _lens_repo_key 同一種算法;code-codex-s1 r1 架構席:兩套 key 並存)
 17533	    key = hashlib.sha256(f"{os.path.realpath(str(repo_root))}|{base_sha}|{head_sha}|schema{_LENS_SCHEMA}".encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()   # 帶 schema 版本:v1.1 舊快取自然 miss(v1.2 r2 外家)
 17534	    return Path.home() / ".cache" / "lumos" / "dispatch-lens" / f"{key}.json"
 17535	
 17536	
 17537	def _lens_cache_read(path, ttl_sec=1200):
 17538	    """只信自己 uid 擁有、group/other 不可寫、TTL 內的檔(r3 載荷席:$TMPDIR 路徑可預測可偽造)。"""
 17539	    import json as _json, stat as _stat, time as _time
 17540	    try:
 17541	        st = path.stat()
 17542	        if st.st_uid != os.getuid() or (st.st_mode & (_stat.S_IWGRP | _stat.S_IWOTH)):
 17543	            return None
 17544	        if _time.time() - st.st_mtime > ttl_sec:
 17545	            return None
 17546	        return _json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
 17547	    except (OSError, ValueError):
 17548	        return None
 17549	
 17550	
 17551	def _lens_cache_write(path, data):
 17552	    """★寫入端也要驗目錄★(2026-09-06 全 repo 審視 #6):原本直接 mkdir + chmod(0700),
 17553	    目錄若被換成指向別處的 symlink,就會把**別人的目錄**權限改掉。讀取端一直都有檢查,
 17554	    寫入端沒有——現在兩邊都走同一支 _trusted_private_dir。不過關就靜默不寫(fail-open)。"""
 17555	    import json as _json, tempfile as _tf
 17556	    try:
 17557	        path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
 17558	        if not _trusted_private_dir(path.parent, ".cache", "lumos", "dispatch-lens"):
 17559	            return
 17560	        os.chmod(path.parent, 0o700)
 17561	        fd, tmp = _tf.mkstemp(dir=str(path.parent), suffix=".tmp")
 17562	        with os.fdopen(fd, "w", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
 17563	            fh.write(_json.dumps(data, ensure_ascii=False))
 17564	        os.chmod(tmp, 0o600)
 17565	        os.replace(tmp, path)
 17566	    except OSError:
 17567	        pass
 17568	
 17569	
 17570	def _lens_contract_lines(text, cap=40):
 17571	    """base 版節點全文裡的合約行:只認 KEY: 行(INVARIANT_RE/CHECKPOINT_RE/IRREVERSIBLE_RE),不掃正文。"""
 17572	    out = []
 17573	    for line in text.split("\n"):
 17574	        s = line.strip()
 17575	        if INVARIANT_RE.match(s) or CHECKPOINT_RE.match(s) or IRREVERSIBLE_RE.match(s):
 17576	            out.append(s[:200])
 17577	            if len(out) >= cap:
 17578	                break
 17579	    return out

 18640	            f.write(json.dumps(event, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")
 18641	    except OSError:
 18642	        pass

 18646	# ── 受波及合約測試真跑閘(Projects/受波及合約測試真跑閘_計劃;design-loop bound-tests-gate-c r1 PASS)────
 18647	# 改到相依功能時,impact 固定席上的合約綁了什麼測試,在 code-loop check(pre-push 每次都呼叫)就真跑一遍;
 18648	# 紅/懸空/方法名不合法 → BLOCKED。掛在「擋」的路徑(check)不掛「記錄」的路徑(pass)——r1 架構席抓的。
 18649	# 假綠三防:①測試名要在該平台真測試集合(同 Check T);②去重鍵=解析後完整指令(同 kill);③方法名重驗同 kill。
 18650	# fail-open 四情境(無 run_cmd / diff 算不出 / 無固定席 / 固定席沒綁)都寫 gate=bound-tests 帳,零觸發看得見。

 18654	def _bound_tests_log(repo_root, kind, nodes, note):
 18655	    """治理帳 gate=bound-tests(獨立 gate,kind 裸結果詞;fail-open,寫不進不影響判定)。"""
 18656	    try:
 18657	        vault = _vault_in(repo_root)
 18658	        if vault is not None:
 18659	            _append_governance_log(vault, [{"gate": "bound-tests", "kind": kind, "hard": kind == "red",
 18660	                                            "nodes": list(nodes), "note": note}])
 18661	    except Exception:
 18662	        pass

 18665	def _bound_tests_for_diff(repo_root, diff_range):
 18666	    """impact 固定席 → 各節點 ★INVARIANT★ 的 [test:] → (node, platform, method, status)。
 18667	    status ∈ real / dangling / fake / bad-name。回 (items, reason_if_none)。"""
 18668	    import io as _io, contextlib as _ctx, json as _json
 18669	    buf = _io.StringIO()
 18670	    try:
 18671	        with _ctx.redirect_stdout(buf), _ctx.redirect_stderr(_io.StringIO()):
 18672	            rc = cmd_impact_diff(diff_range, repo=str(repo_root), as_json=True)
 18673	    except SystemExit:
 18674	        rc = 2
 18675	    except Exception:
 18676	        rc = 2
 18677	    if rc != 0:
 18678	        return [], "diff-unavailable"
 18679	    try:
 18680	        data = _json.loads(buf.getvalue().strip().splitlines()[-1])
 18681	    except Exception:
 18682	        return [], "diff-unavailable"
 18683	    pins = [r["node"] for r in data.get("results", []) if r.get("pinned") and r.get("contract")]
 18684	    if not pins:
 18685	        return [], "no-pins"
 18686	    vault = _vault_in(repo_root)
 18687	    if vault is None:
 18688	        return [], "no-pins"
 18689	    try:
 18690	        env = Env(vault)
 18691	        _, split, default, methods_for, hay_for = _platform_test_index(repo_root)
 18692	    except Exception as e:   # 設定檔壞(多平台沒填 default_platform 等)→ fail-open 記 no-config,不能炸成假 rc1
 18693	        return [], f"no-config:{str(e)[:80]}"
 18694	    items = []
 18695	    for node in pins:
 18696	        n = env.notes.get(node) or env.notes.get(node[:-3] if node.endswith(".md") else node + ".md")
 18697	        if n is None:
 18698	            continue
 18699	        inv, _ = extract_contracts(n)
 18700	        for x in inv:
 18701	            try:
 18702	                pairs = resolve_test_refs(x, split, default)
 18703	            except ValueError as e:
 18704	                items.append((node, "?", str(e)[:60], "bad-name")); continue
 18705	            for plat, method in pairs:
 18706	                if not _KILL_METHOD_OK_RE.fullmatch(method or ""):
 18707	                    items.append((node, plat, method, "bad-name")); continue
 18708	                try:
 18709	                    mset = methods_for(plat)
 18710	                    real = method in mset or ("." in method and method.rsplit(".", 1)[-1] in mset)   # Class.Method 寫法:集合是扁平方法名
 18711	                    fake = (not real) and (method in (hay_for(plat) or ""))
 18712	                except Exception:
 18713	                    real, fake = False, False
 18714	                items.append((node, plat, method, "real" if real else ("fake" if fake else "dangling")))
 18715	    if not items:
 18716	        return [], "no-bound"
 18717	    return items, None
 18718	
 18719	
 18720	def _run_bound_tests(repo_root, items):
 18721	    """逐 (platform, method) 真跑;去重鍵=解析後完整指令。回 results[(node, plat, method, verdict, detail, secs)]。"""
 18722	    import shlex as _shlex, os as _os
 18723	    try:
 18724	        pdata = load_platforms(repo_root)
 18725	    except ValueError as e:
 18726	        return None, f"config platforms 壞: {e}"
 18727	    plats = pdata["platforms"]
 18728	    per_test = float(_os.environ.get("LUMOS_TEST_TIMEOUT", "180") or 0) or None   # 同 runner 變數;0=不限
 18729	    results, seen = [], {}
 18730	    for node, plat, method, status in items:
 18731	        if status != "real":
 18732	            results.append((node, plat, method, "red", {"dangling": "綁的測試在程式碼裡找不到(懸空)",
 18733	                                                       "fake": "綁的名字不是真的測試方法(偽證據)",
 18734	                                                       "bad-name": "測試名不合法或平台前綴未定義"}[status], 0.0))
 18735	            continue
 18736	        pentry = plats.get(plat) or {}
 18737	        run_cmd = pentry.get("run_cmd")
 18738	        if not run_cmd:
 18739	            return None, "no-config"
 18740	        whole = "{method}" not in run_cmd
 18741	        cmd = run_cmd if whole else run_cmd.replace("{method}", _shlex.quote(method))
 18742	        root = str(pentry.get("root") or repo_root)
 18743	        key = (root, cmd)
 18744	        if key in seen:
 18745	            rc, tail, secs, to = seen[key]
 18746	        else:
 18747	            timeout = max(_BOUND_TESTS_WHOLE_SUITE_TIMEOUT, per_test or 0) if whole else per_test
 18748	            rc, tail, secs, to = _kill_run(cmd, root, timeout if timeout else 10 ** 6)
 18749	            seen[key] = (rc, tail, secs, to)
 18750	        if to:
 18751	            results.append((node, plat, method, "red", f"超時(>{int(timeout)}s)", secs))
 18752	        elif rc != 0:
 18753	            results.append((node, plat, method, "red", f"rc={rc} {tail[-120:].strip()}", secs))
 18754	        else:
 18755	            results.append((node, plat, method, "green", "whole-suite" if whole else "", secs))
 18756	    return results, None
 18757	
 18758	
 18759	def _bound_tests_check(repo_root, diff_range, skip=False, skip_note=""):
 18760	    """回 dict{status, ran, red:[…], secs, reason}。status ∈ green/red/skipped/no-config/no-pins/no-bound/diff-unavailable。"""
 18761	    import os as _os
 18762	    if skip or _os.environ.get("LUMOS_SKIP_BOUND_TESTS") == "1":
 18763	        note = skip_note or ("env LUMOS_SKIP_BOUND_TESTS=1" if not skip else "")
 18764	        _bound_tests_log(repo_root, "skipped", [], note)
 18765	        return {"status": "skipped", "ran": 0, "red": [], "secs": 0.0, "reason": note}
 18766	    if str(diff_range or "").startswith("4b825dc642cb6eb9a060e54bf8d69288fbee4904"):
 18767	        # 新分支首推:pre-push 給的是 EMPTY_TREE..sha(整個 repo 當 diff),固定席=全庫合約,跟這次改動無關 → fail-open
 18768	        _bound_tests_log(repo_root, "diff-unavailable", [], "新分支首推無 merge-base,受波及範圍不可算")
 18769	        return {"status": "diff-unavailable", "ran": 0, "red": [], "secs": 0.0, "reason": "新分支首推,受波及範圍不可算(整個 repo 當 diff 沒意義);下次推送就會算"}
 18770	    items, why = _bound_tests_for_diff(repo_root, diff_range)
 18771	    if why:
 18772	        kind = why.split(":", 1)[0]
 18773	        _bound_tests_log(repo_root, kind, [], why if ":" in why else diff_range)
 18774	        return {"status": kind, "ran": 0, "red": [], "secs": 0.0, "reason": why}
 18775	    results, err = _run_bound_tests(repo_root, items)
 18776	    if results is None:
 18777	        _bound_tests_log(repo_root, "no-config", sorted({i[0] for i in items}), err)
 18778	        return {"status": "no-config", "ran": 0, "red": [], "secs": 0.0, "reason": err}
 18779	    red = [(n, p, m, d) for n, p, m, v, d, _ in results if v == "red"]
 18780	    secs = round(sum(r[5] for r in results), 1)
 18781	    ran = sum(1 for r in results if r[3] == "green")
 18782	    if red:
 18783	        _bound_tests_log(repo_root, "red", sorted({r[0] for r in red}), f"{len(red)} 紅/{len(results)}")
 18784	        return {"status": "red", "ran": ran, "red": red, "secs": secs,
 18785	                "reason": "受波及合約的測試沒過:" + ";".join(f"{n} [{m}] {d}" for n, p, m, d in red[:6])}
 18786	    _bound_tests_log(repo_root, "green", sorted({r[0] for r in results}), f"{ran} 綠 {secs}s")
 18787	    return {"status": "green", "ran": ran, "red": [], "secs": secs, "reason": f"受波及合約測試 {ran} 支全綠({secs}s)"}
 18788	
 18789	def _codeloop_guard_verdict(repo_root, diff_range=None, at_sha=None, branch=None, skip_bound=False, skip_note=""):
 18790	    """判定式:tier=high(pitfalls --diff <range> --no-lint --json)
 18791	    AND 無有效 pass/skip 留痕 → blocked=True。
 18792	    留痕座標(prepush範圍修法 #2):at_sha/branch 給了則綁該座標(hook 傳被推送 ref 的
 18793	    local_sha 與 remote_ref 分支),否則讀當前 checkout HEAD/branch(向後相容)。
 18794	    diff_range 給了則跳 merge-base 推導直接算 tier;否則沿 merge-base..HEAD 現行為。
 18795	    fail-open:pitfalls 出錯 / 非 git / 無 merge-base → tier 視作非 high(不 blocked)。
 18796	    回傳 dict{blocked:bool, reason:str, tier:str}。
 18797	    """
 18798	    import json as _json
 18799	    import subprocess as _sp
 18800	
 18801	    # 留痕比對座標:優先用傳入的(hook 綁被推送 ref),否則讀 checkout
 18802	    _codeloop_guard_verdict.last_bound = None   # 每次呼叫先清,提早 fail-open 的分支不會拿到上一輪的殘留
 18803	    marker_sha = at_sha or _codeloop_git_head(repo_root)
 18804	    marker_branch = branch or _codeloop_git_branch(repo_root)
 18805	    if not marker_sha:
 18806	        return {"blocked": False, "reason": "無法取得 HEAD sha(fail-open)", "tier": "unknown"}
 18807	
 18808	    if diff_range is None:
 18809	        # 現行為:merge-base..HEAD 推導(向後相容)
 18810	        head_sha = _codeloop_git_head(repo_root)

# bound-tests-gate

# bound-tests-gate

> 白話:改到相依功能,以前工具只會「點名」那些功能綁的測試,跑不跑靠自律。現在 pre-push 每次呼叫的 `code-loop check` 會把被點名的測試當場跑完:紅的、綁了不存在測試的、名字不合法的,一律擋推送。

## 怎麼跑
1. `impact --diff <range>` 取固定席(合約/事故/直接相依)裡帶合約的節點。
2. 每個節點的 ★INVARIANT★ 行 `[test:…]` 用 `resolve_test_refs` 解平台前綴,再用 Check T 同一套平台真測試索引判存在(real/dangling/fake)。
3. real 的逐支用 `.lumos/config.json` 的 run_cmd 真跑(`_kill_run`,同 kill);去重鍵=完整指令。超時:單支 `LUMOS_TEST_TIMEOUT`(預設 180s,同測試 runner),整套(run_cmd 無 {method})600s。
4. 任一紅 → check 回 BLOCKED(rc1),訊息列合約、測試、尾段輸出,並給 `--skip-bound-tests --note` 範本。

## fail-open 四情境(不擋,但都寫帳讓零觸發看得見)
沒 run_cmd(kind=no-config)/ diff 算不出(diff-unavailable)/ 沒固定席(no-pins)/ 固定席沒綁測試(no-bound)。`gov --stats` 看 bound-tests 那列就知道這道閘在這個專案有沒有真的開。

## 設計迴圈紀錄
`bound-tests-gate-c` r1 四席(通才/正確性邊界/可執行性成本/架構對齊)20 條:19 折 1 放行(hermetic:run_cmd 是專案自宣告指令,與 CI 同信任邊界)。我自己記帳走歪兩次(處置帳一輪只能一筆;每席都要留痕)才過閘,編號 -b/-c 是這樣來的。

## 回頭看條件
- 30 天後 `gov --stats` 若 bound-tests 只有 no-config/no-pins、零 green/red → 入口 hook 加「本專案沒設 run_cmd,合約測試閘沒開」提醒;再 30 天仍零 → 列入只退場不痛的機制檢討。
- 三次 check 超過 3 分鐘 → 加只跑 direct 相依的縮圈選項。
- gov --stats 出現 red 且原因是外部資源 → 加單支白名單跳過。

## 鏡頭側呈現(2026-09-03 v1.1)

`lumos dispatch-lens` 對每條 ★INVARIANT★ 合約行用同一個 `_classify_one` 標「綁定測試:有/懸空/偽證據」或「★裸合約★」,並在段尾固定寫「有=方法存在,不代表跑過或有殺傷力(閘只看 rc)」。鏡頭是參考、本閘是判決:鏡頭讀 base 版合約行、閘讀工作樹版節點,分支改了合約行時兩邊可能不同。單源 [[Projects/派工鏡頭注入_計劃]] v1.1 節。

  3782	def _render_gov_nags(ded, min_days):
  3783	    """機制空轉偵測(Issues/自足性審計提醒空轉四十六天 的出口,2026-08-22):
  3784	    同一道軟閘對同一篇筆記,從第一次提醒到最後一次提醒跨 ≥ min_days 天,而且最後一次提醒就在最近一次
  3785	    doctor 體檢那天(=還在喊、沒人理)。有就列出並回 rc1(給自主迴圈週報當訊號),沒有 rc0。
  3786	    只看 kind=warned 的軟閘;硬擋的本來就擋得住人,不會空轉。"""
  3787	    import datetime as _dt
  3788	    runs = [r["ts"] for r in ded if r.get("gate") == "doctor-run"]
  3789	    last_run = max(runs) if runs else None          # 完整時間戳:同一天處理完再體檢,要能當場消失
  3790	    span = {}   # (gate,node) -> [first_ts,last_ts,count]
  3791	    for r in ded:
  3792	        if r.get("kind") != "warned" or r.get("hard"):
  3793	            continue
  3794	        for nd in r.get("nodes") or []:
  3795	            k = (r["gate"], nd)
  3796	            t = r["ts"]
  3797	            if k not in span:
  3798	                span[k] = [t, t, 1]
  3799	            else:
  3800	                span[k][0] = min(span[k][0], t); span[k][1] = max(span[k][1], t); span[k][2] += 1
  3801	    out = []
  3802	    for (g, nd), (ft, lt, c) in span.items():
  3803	        f, l = ft[:10], lt[:10]
  3804	        try:
  3805	            days = (_dt.date.fromisoformat(l) - _dt.date.fromisoformat(f)).days
  3806	        except ValueError:
  3807	            continue
  3808	        # 「還在喊」= 最後一次提醒不早於最近一次體檢(體檢會先寫提醒再寫 doctor-run,同秒內算還在喊)
  3809	        still = (last_run is None) or (lt[:19] >= last_run[:19])
  3810	        if days >= min_days and still:
  3811	            out.append((days, g, nd, f, l, c))
  3812	    if not out:
  3813	        print(f"沒有空轉的提醒:沒有任何閘對同一篇連續喊超過 {min_days} 天還沒人理。")
  3814	        return 0
  3815	    out.sort(reverse=True)
  3816	    print(f"提醒:下面這些是「同一道閘對同一篇連續喊了 ≥{min_days} 天、最近一次體檢({(last_run or '?')[:16]})還在喊」的空轉清單,共 {len(out)} 組。")
  3817	    print("機制有在跑,只是沒人去看——要嘛處理那篇,要嘛承認這道提醒對它無效、把它降成不提醒。")
  3818	    for days, g, nd, f, l, c in out[:30]:
  3819	        print(f"  {days:>4} 天  {g:10} {nd}  ({f} → {l},喊了 {c} 次)")
  3820	    if len(out) > 30:
  3821	        print(f"  … 還有 {len(out) - 30} 組")
  3822	    print("處理完一篇後它自然從清單消失;要看某篇被哪幾道閘喊:lumos gov <篇名>")
  3823	    return 1
  3824

 18646	# ── 受波及合約測試真跑閘(Projects/受波及合約測試真跑閘_計劃;design-loop bound-tests-gate-c r1 PASS)────
 18647	# 改到相依功能時,impact 固定席上的合約綁了什麼測試,在 code-loop check(pre-push 每次都呼叫)就真跑一遍;
 18648	# 紅/懸空/方法名不合法 → BLOCKED。掛在「擋」的路徑(check)不掛「記錄」的路徑(pass)——r1 架構席抓的。

 18651	_BOUND_TESTS_WHOLE_SUITE_TIMEOUT = 600.0   # 同 kill baseline 的下限

 18654	def _bound_tests_log(repo_root, kind, nodes, note):
 18655	    """治理帳 gate=bound-tests(獨立 gate,kind 裸結果詞;fail-open,寫不進不影響判定)。"""
 18656	    try:
 18657	        vault = _vault_in(repo_root)
 18658	        if vault is not None:
 18659	            _append_governance_log(vault, [{"gate": "bound-tests", "kind": kind, "hard": kind == "red",
 18660	                                            "nodes": list(nodes), "note": note}])
 18661	    except Exception:
 18662	        pass

 18665	def _bound_tests_for_diff(repo_root, diff_range):
 18666	    """impact 固定席 → 各節點 ★INVARIANT★ 的 [test:] → (node, platform, method, status)。
 18667	    status ∈ real / dangling / fake / bad-name。回 (items, reason_if_none)。"""
 18668	    import io as _io, contextlib as _ctx, json as _json
 18669	    buf = _io.StringIO()
 18670	    try:
 18671	        with _ctx.redirect_stdout(buf), _ctx.redirect_stderr(_io.StringIO()):
 18672	            rc = cmd_impact_diff(diff_range, repo=str(repo_root), as_json=True)
 18673	    except SystemExit:
 18674	        rc = 2
 18675	    except Exception:
 18676	        rc = 2
 18677	    if rc != 0:
 18678	        return [], "diff-unavailable"
 18679	    try:
 18680	        data = _json.loads(buf.getvalue().strip().splitlines()[-1])
 18681	    except Exception:
 18682	        return [], "diff-unavailable"
 18683	    pins = [r["node"] for r in data.get("results", []) if r.get("pinned") and r.get("contract")]
 18684	    if not pins:
 18685	        return [], "no-pins"
 18686	    vault = _vault_in(repo_root)
 18687	    if vault is None:
 18688	        return [], "no-pins"
 18689	    try:
 18690	        env = Env(vault)
 18691	        _, split, default, methods_for, hay_for = _platform_test_index(repo_root)
 18692	    except Exception as e:   # 設定檔壞(多平台沒填 default_platform 等)→ fail-open 記 no-config,不能炸成假 rc1
 18693	        return [], f"no-config:{str(e)[:80]}"
 18694	    items = []
 18695	    for node in pins:
 18696	        n = env.notes.get(node) or env.notes.get(node[:-3] if node.endswith(".md") else node + ".md")
 18697	        if n is None:
 18698	            continue
 18699	        inv, _ = extract_contracts(n)
 18700	        for x in inv:
 18701	            try:
 18702	                pairs = resolve_test_refs(x, split, default)
 18703	            except ValueError as e:
 18704	                items.append((node, "?", str(e)[:60], "bad-name")); continue
 18705	            for plat, method in pairs:
 18706	                if not _KILL_METHOD_OK_RE.fullmatch(method or ""):
 18707	                    items.append((node, plat, method, "bad-name")); continue
 18708	                try:
 18709	                    mset = methods_for(plat)
 18710	                    real = method in mset or ("." in method and method.rsplit(".", 1)[-1] in mset)   # Class.Method 寫法:集合是扁平方法名
 18711	                    fake = (not real) and (method in (hay_for(plat) or ""))
 18712	                except Exception:
 18713	                    real, fake = False, False
 18714	                items.append((node, plat, method, "real" if real else ("fake" if fake else "dangling")))
 18715	    if not items:
 18716	        return [], "no-bound"
 18717	    return items, None
 18718	
 18719	
 18720	def _run_bound_tests(repo_root, items):
 18721	    """逐 (platform, method) 真跑;去重鍵=解析後完整指令。回 results[(node, plat, method, verdict, detail, secs)]。"""
 18722	    import shlex as _shlex, os as _os
 18723	    try:
 18724	        pdata = load_platforms(repo_root)
 18725	    except ValueError as e:
 18726	        return None, f"config platforms 壞: {e}"
 18727	    plats = pdata["platforms"]
 18728	    per_test = float(_os.environ.get("LUMOS_TEST_TIMEOUT", "180") or 0) or None   # 同 runner 變數;0=不限
 18729	    results, seen = [], {}
 18730	    for node, plat, method, status in items:
 18731	        if status != "real":
 18732	            results.append((node, plat, method, "red", {"dangling": "綁的測試在程式碼裡找不到(懸空)",
 18733	                                                       "fake": "綁的名字不是真的測試方法(偽證據)",
 18734	                                                       "bad-name": "測試名不合法或平台前綴未定義"}[status], 0.0))
 18735	            continue
 18736	        pentry = plats.get(plat) or {}
 18737	        run_cmd = pentry.get("run_cmd")
 18738	        if not run_cmd:
 18739	            return None, "no-config"
 18740	        whole = "{method}" not in run_cmd
 18741	        cmd = run_cmd if whole else run_cmd.replace("{method}", _shlex.quote(method))
 18742	        root = str(pentry.get("root") or repo_root)
 18743	        key = (root, cmd)
 18744	        if key in seen:
 18745	            rc, tail, secs, to = seen[key]
 18746	        else:
 18747	            timeout = max(_BOUND_TESTS_WHOLE_SUITE_TIMEOUT, per_test or 0) if whole else per_test
 18748	            rc, tail, secs, to = _kill_run(cmd, root, timeout if timeout else 10 ** 6)
 18749	            seen[key] = (rc, tail, secs, to)
 18750	        if to:
 18751	            results.append((node, plat, method, "red", f"超時(>{int(timeout)}s)", secs))
 18752	        elif rc != 0:
 18753	            results.append((node, plat, method, "red", f"rc={rc} {tail[-120:].strip()}", secs))
 18754	        else:
 18755	            results.append((node, plat, method, "green", "whole-suite" if whole else "", secs))
 18756	    return results, None
 18757	
 18758	
 18759	def _bound_tests_check(repo_root, diff_range, skip=False, skip_note=""):
 18760	    """回 dict{status, ran, red:[…], secs, reason}。status ∈ green/red/skipped/no-config/no-pins/no-bound/diff-unavailable。"""
 18761	    import os as _os
 18762	    if skip or _os.environ.get("LUMOS_SKIP_BOUND_TESTS") == "1":
 18763	        note = skip_note or ("env LUMOS_SKIP_BOUND_TESTS=1" if not skip else "")
 18764	        _bound_tests_log(repo_root, "skipped", [], note)
 18765	        return {"status": "skipped", "ran": 0, "red": [], "secs": 0.0, "reason": note}
 18766	    if str(diff_range or "").startswith("4b825dc642cb6eb9a060e54bf8d69288fbee4904"):
 18767	        # 新分支首推:pre-push 給的是 EMPTY_TREE..sha(整個 repo 當 diff),固定席=全庫合約,跟這次改動無關 → fail-open
 18768	        _bound_tests_log(repo_root, "diff-unavailable", [], "新分支首推無 merge-base,受波及範圍不可算")
 18769	        return {"status": "diff-unavailable", "ran": 0, "red": [], "secs": 0.0, "reason": "新分支首推,受波及範圍不可算(整個 repo 當 diff 沒意義);下次推送就會算"}
 18770	    items, why = _bound_tests_for_diff(repo_root, diff_range)
 18771	    if why:
 18772	        kind = why.split(":", 1)[0]
 18773	        _bound_tests_log(repo_root, kind, [], why if ":" in why else diff_range)
 18774	        return {"status": kind, "ran": 0, "red": [], "secs": 0.0, "reason": why}
 18775	    results, err = _run_bound_tests(repo_root, items)
 18776	    if results is None:
 18777	        _bound_tests_log(repo_root, "no-config", sorted({i[0] for i in items}), err)
 18778	        return {"status": "no-config", "ran": 0, "red": [], "secs": 0.0, "reason": err}
 18779	    red = [(n, p, m, d) for n, p, m, v, d, _ in results if v == "red"]

計劃自己承認會漏，卻把既有硬合約退化寫成取捨：

file:line：`docs/lumos-toolchain-knowledge/Projects/合約測試閘什麼時候跑_計劃.md:141`  
引句:「原本會把間接相依也算進來,新做法只算直接點名」

等 CI 日後紅才回補，代表已先放行本機本應攔下的破壞，不能作為硬合約閘的設計出口。

### F2：whole-suite 分流缺少可實作的 tier 輸入與落點

severity: major  
blocking:是  
file:line：`docs/lumos-toolchain-knowledge/Projects/合約測試閘什麼時候跑_計劃.md:89`  
引句:「每次推送整套跑不可接受,所以這種情況維持只在高風險推送跑」

計劃沒有定義這項判斷位於四步中的哪一步，也沒有定義 tier 如何傳入新入口：

- S2 的 CLI 只有 `bound-tests check --diff <範圍> [--json]`，見計劃 `:71`，沒有 `--tier` 或 `--allow-whole-suite`。
- S2 又要求每個 ref 都呼叫且「跟風險分級完全正交」，見計劃 `:72`。
- 現行 whole-suite 只能在取得命中項目後，由 `_run_bound_tests` 查看 `"{method}" not in run_cmd` 才知道，見 `scripts/lumos:18737-18748`。
- 現行 hook 的 tier 只存在 `pitfalls` 分支內，見 `scripts/hooks/pre-push:141-155`。

照字面實作會產生兩種不同結果：獨立入口不知道 tier 而每次跑整套，或為避免熱路徑成本而一律 deferred；兩者都違反 S4。設計必須釘死順序及介面，例如第一步讀設定時先分類可過濾性，命中節點後由 hook 明確傳入已算出的 tier，只有 `tier=high` 才准進 whole-suite。

### F3：「個位數」的成本前提已失真，但不是效能 blocker

severity: minor  
blocking:否  
file:line：`docs/lumos-toolchain-knowledge/Projects/合約測試閘什麼時候跑_計劃.md:62`  
引句:「有綁測試的節點是個位數,交集是集合運算」

實測目前是 432 篇節點，其中：

- 綁測試節點：10 篇，不是個位數
- 綁測試合約行：25 條
- 已抽出的正文路徑：48 個

數字雖錯，但方案的純成本確實便宜：載入後掃合約約 1.01 ms、十篇正文抽路徑約 4.12 ms、2354×48 的集合交集平均約 1.77 μs。應修正文案與基準，不構成否決理由。

### 其餘實測結論

- S3 首推成立：`pre-push` 已從 stdin 取得 `local_sha`，新 ref 轉成 `EMPTY_TREE..local_sha`，見 `scripts/hooks/pre-push:24-31,127-135`。實跑 `git diff --name-only EMPTY_TREE..HEAD` 取得 2354 檔，約 0.02 秒。
- S6 改動面不大：治理帳 mapper 已保留 `kind`，且去重鍵已包含 `gate + kind`，見 `scripts/lumos:3859-3861,3898`。主要是 `_render_gov_stats` 增加 bound-tests 子分類與測試；`gov --stats` 現行約 0.43 秒。只要把子表限定在 `gate=bound-tests`，不必改其他閘的既有總表欄位。

### F1：反向直連會讓既有硬合約測試消失

severity: blocker  
blocking:是  
file:line：`docs/lumos-toolchain-knowledge/Projects/合約測試閘什麼時候跑_計劃.md:59`  
引句:「拿那一小撮有綁測試的節點,問它們點名哪些檔,跟這次改的檔取交集」

這不是可接受的召回率取捨，而是已重現的合約退化：

- 取真實範圍 `59c8b9..34bdad`，舊 `impact --diff` 把 `Systems/bound-tests-gate.md` 列為 `indirect`、`pinned:true`、`contract:INVARIANT`，來源是 `scripts/lumos`，所以會跑 `[test:t_bound_tests_gate]`。
- 新設計按正文路徑反查時，該節點得到的檔案集合是空的，改 `scripts/lumos` 不會命中，測試不再跑。
- 間接關係確實來自既有一跳圖邊：`Projects/派工鏡頭注入_計劃.md → Systems/bound-tests-gate.md`。舊實作明確保留間接固定席，見 `scripts/lumos:17162`；既有取測試入口則接收全部 pinned contract，見 `scripts/lumos:18683`。

而且第三條路其實已經存在：`about_code` 正是「節點主要在講哪些程式檔」的正規欄位。該節點已列 `.github/workflows/ci.yml`、`scripts/hooks/pre-push`、`scripts/lumos`，見節點 frontmatter；既有載入函式 `_impact_about_counts` 在 `scripts/lumos:16771`。本案應以 `about_code ∩ changed_files` 做便宜直連，再明確決定如何保留至少一跳關係，不能用正文 inline-code 取代語意欄位。

計劃自己承認會漏，卻把既有硬合約退化寫成取捨：

file:line：`docs/lumos-toolchain-knowledge/Projects/合約測試閘什麼時候跑_計劃.md:141`  
引句:「原本會把間接相依也算進來,新做法只算直接點名」

等 CI 日後紅才回補，代表已先放行本機本應攔下的破壞，不能作為硬合約閘的設計出口。
